Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the graphs framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 997. The resulting operator achieves bound O(177/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 997 + O(1/n^177). Summing over all modes and applying the graphs convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
